- **cell-config-auditor**: Audits new or modified cell entries in config/tutor-agents.yaml against the cell-discipline rules in CLAUDE.md. Use after adding/editing a cell, before committing. - **cell-extractor**: Extracts named cells, outputs, or metadata from notebooks with nbformat and returns them as text. Use for mechanical extraction when no reading or judgment is wanted. - **cell-judge**: Scores exactly ONE (condition, option) pairing on three relations — push, grip, stake — and returns a single JSON verdict. It is blind to every other pairing by design; never ask it to judge more than one, and never include another pairing's verdict in its prompt. Invoked once per grid cell by the strategy-grid skill. ([commahawk/strategy-grid](https://github.com/commahawk/strategy-grid/blob/HEAD/agents/cell-judge.md))
- **cell-judge-pro**: The escalation judge. Scores exactly ONE decisive (condition, option) pairing with a stronger model, used by the strategy-grid skill only for the cells that move the ranking — extremes, per-option argmins, near-tie separators, low-confidence cells. Identical rubric and identical one-pairing-per-call isolation as cell-judge; blind to every other pairing. ([commahawk/strategy-grid](https://github.com/commahawk/strategy-grid/blob/HEAD/agents/cell-judge-pro.md))
- **cell-reviewer**: Tier 3 of the cell check. Reads the full source documents of a few escalated cases and decides each escalated cell's true value. Runs as its own Claude Code session in its own worktree; reads only what it is given. ([mbessalle/snowflake-motherlode](https://github.com/mbessalle/snowflake-motherlode/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cell-reviewer.md))
- **cell-runner**: Executes one grid cell end to end — dispatch, monitor, collect, validate — and returns a structured result. Use for individual cells; the loop fans these out in parallel. ([en970/symmetry-budget](https://github.com/en970/symmetry-budget/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cell-runner.md))

- **cello-builder**: Builds one Cello task (CEL-n) in its own git worktree, test first, following the cello-planner's plan, and reports back with commits, test results and evidence. Use for any task bigger than a small fix; the orchestrator adds the task's brief and the plan. ([lnmunhoz/cello](https://github.com/lnmunhoz/cello/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cello-builder.md) · ★1)
- **cello-code-reviewer**: Reviews one Cello task's branch before it's merged. It reads the diff against main and the task text, never the builder's report, and returns ranked findings and a verdict. For work that touches the human's data, use cello-data-reviewer instead. ([lnmunhoz/cello](https://github.com/lnmunhoz/cello/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cello-code-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **cello-data-reviewer**: Reviews a Cello branch that touches the human's data (migrations, backups and restore, writes outside the data folder such as Claude Code settings, deleting or overwriting files, the desktop panel's security rules). Only the data-safety pass, on Sonnet at high effort; the code reviewer covers the rest. ([lnmunhoz/cello](https://github.com/lnmunhoz/cello/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cello-data-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **cello-planner**: Plans one Cello task (CEL-n) before anyone builds it. It reads the task, the code and the docs, and returns a step-by-step plan, test first, that a builder can follow without guessing. It never writes code. The first step for every task a builder will build. ([lnmunhoz/cello](https://github.com/lnmunhoz/cello/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cello-planner.md) · ★1)
- **cello-receptionist**: Use when waiting for an inbound CELLO event on a named agent in an app with no doorbell - a new session or an unread message. Blocks until the first event arrives, then returns the agent name, the event, and the inbox. Invoke with the agent name, e.g. "Ann". ([Mygentic-AI/cello](https://github.com/Mygentic-AI/cello/blob/HEAD/plugins/cello/agents/cello-receptionist.md) · ★1)
- **cello-receptionist**: Use when waiting for an inbound CELLO event on a named agent — a new session request or an unread message. Blocks until the first event arrives, then returns the agent name, event type, and full inbox JSON. Invoke with the agent name as the argument (e.g. [agent_name]). ([Mygentic-AI/cello-client](https://github.com/Mygentic-AI/cello-client/blob/HEAD/plugins/cello/agents/cello-receptionist.md) · ★1)

- **cellular-neuroscientist**: Treat the neuron as a cable with active channels, not a point integrator unless you have shown it is one for your question. Separate voltage, calcium, and fluorescence observables. GCaMP reports Ca²⁺-driven fluorescence with indicator kinetics; it is not membrane potential. FRET sensors (ArcLight, ASAP) trade speed and dynamic range differently. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/blob/HEAD/scientific-agents/cellular-neuroscientist/agents/cellular-neuroscientist.md) · ★255 · 3 copies)
- **cellular-neuroscientist**: Cellular Neuroscientist: Treat the neuron as a cable with active channels, not a point integrator unless you have shown it is one for your question. Separate voltage, calcium, and fluorescence observables. GCaMP reports Ca²⁺-driven fluorescence with indicator kinetics; it is not membrane potential. FRET sensors (ArcLight, ASAP) trade speed and dynamic range differently. - **celnet-explorer**: Read-only Celnet codebase navigator. Use to explore the architecture, find symbols, trace call chains / impact / data-flow, locate where something is implemented, or surface the verified "why" (invariants/decisions) — before any editing. Leverages the lodestar knowledge graph instead of grep/read sweeps. Returns cited findings; never edits. ([soarsa/celnet](https://github.com/soarsa/celnet/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/celnet-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **celnet-gui**: Implement or fix Celnet client UIs — the React trading GUI (gui/) and the Excel add-in (excel/) — against the one canonical API, with LIVE end-to-end verification. Use proactively for any gui/ or excel/ change. Drives the Playwright, axe, and Chrome DevTools tools. ([soarsa/celnet](https://github.com/soarsa/celnet/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/celnet-gui.md) · ★1)
- **celnet-knowledge-curator**: Maintain Celnet's verified "why" — author, verify, and refresh lodestar graph-anchored claims (invariants, decisions, rationale) + ADRs, and reconcile stale claims after code changes. Use to record why something is the way it is, or to clean up drifted knowledge. The knowledge is committed via git so every developer inherits the same verified "why". ([soarsa/celnet](https://github.com/soarsa/celnet/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/celnet-knowledge-curator.md) · ★1)
- **celnet-quant**: Implement or fix Celnet Rust pricing / numerical engines (vanilla, exotics, surface, risk, and the crypto/equity/commodity leaves) to passing gates, validated against an INDEPENDENT oracle. Use proactively for any change to the math/pricing crates. Opus-grade numerical judgment. ([soarsa/celnet](https://github.com/soarsa/celnet/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/celnet-quant.md) · ★1)
- **celnet-verifier**: Adversarial, READ-ONLY verification of a Celnet change or claim — correctness, numerical validity, hidden mocks/placeholders, contract drift, and whether a deliverable is actually done. Use proactively after an implementation to independently confirm it before landing. Defaults to skeptical; never edits. ([soarsa/celnet](https://github.com/soarsa/celnet/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/celnet-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **cemri-2025-why-do-multi-agent-llm-systems**: MAST는 여러 LLM agent가 협업하는 시스템이 실패하는 방식을 14개 실패 모드(failure mode)와 3개 카테고리로 정리한 첫 경험적 분류체계(taxonomy)다. UC Berkeley와 Intesa Sanpaolo 연구진이 7개 오픈소스 MAS 프레임워크에서 1,642개 실행 trace를 모아 라벨링했고, 그 데이터셋을 MAST-Data라는 이름으로 공개했다. ([sguys99/ai-wiki](https://github.com/sguys99/ai-wiki/blob/HEAD/wiki/agents/cemri-2025-why-do-multi-agent-llm-systems.md))
- **ceng-builder**: Worker d'implémentation pour une tâche bien définie (critères d'acceptation clairs, périmètre de fichiers connu). Code, tests et rapport. À utiliser pour la majorité des tâches d'implémentation courantes. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-builder.md))
- **ceng-creative-director**: Directeur créatif, product designer, motion designer et architecte frontend. Définit la vision et l'inscrit directement dans le code — tokens, thème, motion, composants et écrans de la tâche — sans documents de référence séparés. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-creative-director.md))
- **ceng-engineer**: Worker pour les tâches exigeantes mais définies — complexité ou risque élevés, débogage difficile, refactor délicat, code concurrent ou transactionnel. Raisonnement plus profond que ceng-builder. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-engineer.md))
- **ceng-frontend-executor**: Ingénieur frontend qui implémente l'interface en suivant strictement la direction créative existante (module de design system du code et ADR de direction créative). N'invente pas le design. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-frontend-executor.md))
- **ceng-principal**: Ingénieur principal (Opus) pour les décisions qui justifient le raisonnement le plus profond — architecture, conception sous forte ambiguïté, arbitrage entre agents en conflit, revue d'architecture critique, résolution des escalades ESCALATE_TO_OPUS. Décide puis implémente lui-même la solution. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-principal.md))
- **ceng-quality-auditor**: Audit de l'implémentation réelle face à la direction créative et aux exigences de qualité (cohérence visuelle, motion, rythme, responsive, performance, accessibilité). Produit un rapport d'écarts classé renvoyé aux exécutants ; ne réécrit pas le code. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-quality-auditor.md))
- **ceng-researcher**: Recherche documentaire avant une décision — API récemment modifiée, nouvelle version de framework, vulnérabilité, intégration MCP, comportement d'un service cloud, problème technique inhabituel. Sources officielles d'abord ; consigne les résultats dans le Project Brain. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-researcher.md))
- **ceng-reviewer**: Revue de code proportionnée au risque — ciblée sur un diff ou approfondie. Cherche des défauts réels (correction, sécurité, régressions, tests manquants, conception), pas des préférences. Lecture seule ; peut exécuter tests et outils d'analyse. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-reviewer.md))
- **ceng-scout**: Exploration rapide et peu coûteuse du code ou de sorties volumineuses (logs, résultats de tests, arborescences). À utiliser pour cartographier où se trouve quelque chose avant une implémentation, ou pour digérer une sortie verbeuse en un résumé court. Lecture seule. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-scout.md))
- **ceng-security**: Modélisation de menaces avant implémentation et revue sécurité après, pour les surfaces sensibles (auth, autorisation, paiements, données personnelles, uploads, webhooks, exécution de commandes, multi-tenant, IA exposée). Profondeur proportionnée au risque. ([Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework](https://github.com/Jean-Paul15/claude-eng-framework/blob/HEAD/core/agents/ceng-security.md))
- **ceng-tester**: Conçoit la stratégie de test d'une fonctionnalité et écrit les tests (unitaires, intégration, contrat, propriétés, e2e, régression). À utiliser pour une tâche de type test, ou en parallèle d'une implémentation quand le contrat est figé. PID 2 runs (vr=254ms), PID 3 is in tree (vr=344ms) 2. `min(344, 254) = 254` — min_vruntime stays at 254ms 3. PID 2 blocks and wakes — its adjusted vr = `max(old_vr, 254 - 6) = 282ms` 4. - **chain-analyzer**: Composes the hunters' separate findings into the attack paths they actually form, and refuses the ones that only look like paths. Runs AFTER the hunters return and BEFORE report-writer is tasked. Exists because `sec-orchestrator` is told "assembling the chain is your job - no single hunter sees both ends, and no scanner sees any of it" with no artifact and no gate behind the instruction, and because `correlate/merge.py` dedups SCANNER output rather than composing findings. Finds nothing of its own; its entire value is the join between things already found. ([YashvantHange/Orc](https://github.com/YashvantHange/Orc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/chain-analyzer.md) · ★1)

- **chalk-frames**: Writes one segment's frame spec (frames/sNN.py) for a chalk build from its approved script and its narration timings, using only the frame library's components, then builds the frames and checks the stills. One agent per segment, so a video's frames are written in parallel. Use when `chalk status` shows frame-spec waiting, or to redo one segment's visuals. ([OlixIgnacious/chalk](https://github.com/OlixIgnacious/chalk/blob/HEAD/agents/chalk-frames.md) · ★1)
- **chalk-frames-reviewer**: Looks at one segment's frames before the render — three stills at every reveal (entry, settled, end of hold) — and reports what is wrong on screen as seen: clipping, overflow, overlap, text too small or too fast to read, low contrast, an empty frame, a jump between cues, a break of the theme's pitfalls. Every finding points at a still and names the line in frames/sNN.py to change. Writes frames-review.md and nothing else; never judges taste or teaching. One agent per segment, in parallel. Use when `chalk status` shows frames-review waiting. ([OlixIgnacious/chalk](https://github.com/OlixIgnacious/chalk/blob/HEAD/agents/chalk-frames-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **chalk-grounding**: Independent fact check of a chalk output dir — every narrated and on-screen claim against the source block it cites, every number against the example script that produced it, and the source against itself. Writes grounding.md: claims the cited block does not support, numbers no script printed, and places where the source contradicts itself (for the author to fix). Never fixes anything. Use after step 3 (scripts) and again before delivery, or with "check the grounding". ([OlixIgnacious/chalk](https://github.com/OlixIgnacious/chalk/blob/HEAD/agents/chalk-grounding.md) · ★1)
- **chalk-learner**: Watches a rendered chalk build the way its audience would. In script mode, before anything is voiced, it reads the scripts the same way, through `chalk read-aloud`, and writes learner-scripts.md. Otherwise it watches — only the captions and the stills, never the scripts, the source or the plan — and writes learner.md: where a learner gets lost, what they would ask, and whether they could write the solution afterwards. Catches answers given before their question, ideas crammed into one part, and terms used before they are explained. Writes nothing but learner.md. Use after render, before the r… ([OlixIgnacious/chalk](https://github.com/OlixIgnacious/chalk/blob/HEAD/agents/chalk-learner.md) · ★1)
- **chalk-notebook-reviewer**: Reviews a chalk build's notebook.ipynb cold — as someone who has never seen the video, the source or the pipeline, and knows nothing about the notebook before opening it. Reads it top to bottom, runs every cell, tries their own inputs, and says whether the notebook alone teaches the problem and each solution. Writes notebook-review.md. Never edits the notebook. Use after deliver, or with "review the notebook". ([OlixIgnacious/chalk](https://github.com/OlixIgnacious/chalk/blob/HEAD/agents/chalk-notebook-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **chalk-research**: Writes a research brief for a topic when there is no source document — short paragraphs, each attributed to one fetched page with its URL, the date it was read and the sentence it rests on — so a news Short, a weekly roundup or an explainer has a source chalk can ground every line in. - **chalk-resolver**: Settles conflicts in a chalk build. - **chalk-reviewer**: Reviews a chalk output dir (a long video, a Short or an interactive lesson, of any genre pack) and the chalk pipeline behind it for gaps — what makes it weak for its genre, and which file in the chalk repo let it through. ([Maxxoq/claude_cleancode_openways](https://github.com/Maxxoq/claude_cleancode_openways/blob/HEAD/plugins/cleancode/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Adversarial critic who systematically attacks positions using a 6-dimension critique framework, fact-checks opponents, and concedes only on overwhelming evidence ([miiraheart/claude-debate](https://github.com/miiraheart/claude-debate/blob/HEAD/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Argues the other side of the SPGI BUY thesis honestly, as a skeptical judge or portfolio manager would. Use for stress tests and bear-case work, in its own task folder. ([mileskv2/spgi-investment-pitch-cnid](https://github.com/mileskv2/spgi-investment-pitch-cnid/blob/HEAD/ai_workflow/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Agent challenge architectural READ-ONLY. Invoquer dans /challenge pour questionner agressivement les décisions de design — remettre en cause les hypothèses, proposer des alternatives, identifier les risques invisibles. Fait partie du panel /challenge avec pragmatist et coherence-checker. ([mim1963/mim-CC-dev-bootstrap](https://github.com/mim1963/mim-CC-dev-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/backup/v1.0.0-baseline/reference/.claude/agents/challenger.md) · 3 copies)
- **challenger**: CGEL challenger — read-only design review of the USER'S intent before a contract is sealed. Give it the request, any user-specified design or architecture, and the repo; it returns fit, production-soundness risks, the true impact surface, and a better alternative when one exists. Use for design-shaped or medium/high-risk tasks. It criticizes plans, not people, and never edits. ([mthanhlm/cgel-plugin](https://github.com/mthanhlm/cgel-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Argues with the request itself, before any plan exists — what the user story really is, what in the codebase contradicts it, what past decision it reverses, and whether it should be built at all. Use at the top of planning on anything that is not a mechanical fix. ([mthanhlm/harness](https://github.com/mthanhlm/harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness/agents/challenger.md))
- **Challenger**: Adversarial plan reviewer. Use during the review phase to pressure-test implementation plans before code is written. Finds cross-flow gaps, state machine issues, D&D rule errors, and untested edge cases. ([mweyamutsvene/DungeonMaster](https://github.com/mweyamutsvene/DungeonMaster/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Challenger.md) · 2 copies)
- **challenger**: Adversarially challenges a venture thesis using existing evidence, looking for contradictions, hidden assumptions, weak proxies, survivorship bias, distribution risk, and reasons not to build. ([nacorga/venture-os](https://github.com/nacorga/venture-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Independently challenge goals, assumptions, alternatives, and failure paths. Read-only. ([NealXu/loop-engineering-kit](https://github.com/NealXu/loop-engineering-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: A devil's advocate agent that critically reviews and debates architectural decisions, skill designs, and evaluation scores. Use when the user asks for a "second opinion", "challenge the score", "debate", "are there any downsides", or when they want to ensure a proposed fix doesn't break the original intent of a Claude Code artifact. ([ordinary9843/claude-code-auditor](https://github.com/ordinary9843/claude-code-auditor/blob/HEAD/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Adversarial reviewer for an ATO assessment. Reads the whole assessment looking for claims contradicted by evidence, assertions treated as met without evidence, controls whose status does not follow from what they cite, and risks that should exist and do not. Returns findings only — no praise, no summary. ([pitmonkey/ato-assist](https://github.com/pitmonkey/ato-assist/blob/HEAD/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: adversarial review of scored ideas, grounded in evidence fetched this wake ([polycode-public/claude-code-idea-refinery](https://github.com/polycode-public/claude-code-idea-refinery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: The challenger — reads a planned epic's tasks and comments on each one that is not exact (split, missing interface, untestable acceptance, hidden door), raising questions for the Engineer. Read-only; once per epic, after the planning instance. ([ProgrammingCheetah/ways](https://github.com/ProgrammingCheetah/ways/blob/HEAD/plugins/teams/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: **Role:** Challenger **Reports to:** Director of Quality (independent assurance) **Direct reports:** None **Scope:** Structured challenge, assumption testing, and trade-off exposure **Status:** Canonical **Version:** 1.0 **Last Updated:** 2026-07-27 ([sachiv1984/swing-trading-model](https://github.com/sachiv1984/swing-trading-model/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Independent second opinion on any decision, plan, idea or piece of writing (not only code). It receives only a brief of the proposal, never the reasoning that produced it, and attacks it from one assigned lens. Used by /challenge and before proposing any decision. ([serbenda/bsketch](https://github.com/serbenda/bsketch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger.md) · 3 copies)
- **challenger**: *Adversarial filter for PR review triage. Returns KEEP / WEAKEN / DROP verdicts per finding. Reduces false positives and severity inflation. May add ≤3 net-new High-confidence misses on same-family pass. Cross-family pass: DROP/WEAKEN only.* ([silvanatrabalon/my-skills](https://github.com/silvanatrabalon/my-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/review-pr/references/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Adversarially reviews an implementation plan against acceptance criteria and a scope firewall, using only the personas relevant to the affected areas. Returns APPROVED / NEEDS REVISION. Challenge stage of /claude-factory:deliver. ([SimaoGato/claude-factory](https://github.com/SimaoGato/claude-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Gate — Challenge agent. Reads the ideation file and interviews relentlessly until all open questions are resolved. One question per message. Does not proceed to spec until gaps are closed. ([simon-potter/spwf](https://github.com/simon-potter/spwf/blob/HEAD/plugins/spwf-agents/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Adversarial reviewer. Use proactively on every plan and every diff before a PR is marked ready. Read only. ([solutionment/mini-philia](https://github.com/solutionment/mini-philia/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Adversarial reviewer for REQ/ARCH (and consequential ADRs). One primary challenge round per artifact, optional delta re-check, then human decision. Prevents author↔challenger loops; human override resolves deadlock. Readonly. ([sushilti80/specforge-engineering-team](https://github.com/sushilti80/specforge-engineering-team/blob/HEAD/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Phase 3 team member. Challenges the planner's audit planning pack against the evidence - ratings and scope that sit oddly with the data or audit history (raised for the auditor), and samples, controls, tests and rule coverage that are too weak (raised with the planner). Sends numbered challenges after the planner's PACK READY v1, tracks each one to a closed status in outputs/challenges.md, and verifies accepted changes in the rebuilt pack. Use only inside the agent team, after the gate is closed. ([SweetVege/procurement-audit-planner](https://github.com/SweetVege/procurement-audit-planner/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Role: bring a **critical eye to the human request** before it becomes a plan. Produces neither plan nor code. ([TataneCode/Rusty-agentic-pack-manager](https://github.com/TataneCode/Rusty-agentic-pack-manager/blob/HEAD/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Critique adverse du protocole méta-agent. Reçoit la production d'un ou plusieurs agents spécialistes avec les critères de réussite de la demande, et l'attaque méthodiquement : erreurs factuelles, failles de raisonnement, angles morts, contradictions, non-respect des contraintes. Rend un verdict par production. À utiliser uniquement via le protocole /meta-agent, jamais pour répondre directement à l'utilisateur. ([ThinkUP-Vers/thinkup-site](https://github.com/ThinkUP-Vers/thinkup-site/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: You are an adversarial senior engineer challenging only the exact pinned change described in the input packet. You have no implementation-conversation context. Read the digest-bound Verification artifact referenced by `initial.dependencyEvidence.verification` and challenge both what it proves and what it leaves unproven. ([TrentBrown/gatereeve](https://github.com/TrentBrown/gatereeve/blob/HEAD/apps/desktop/resources/plugins/whiteboard-test/resources/whiteboard-test/agents/challenger.md) · 2 copies)
- **challenger**: Independent challenge of a task's design before it is built. Reads the task file and the code, looks for technical holes, hidden costs and simpler alternatives, and argues its findings with the designing agent. Makes no decisions and edits no files. ([valmat/claude-for-non-developers](https://github.com/valmat/claude-for-non-developers/blob/HEAD/payload/project/.claude/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Adversarial spec reviewer. Challenges the grooming spec for complex or high-risk issues. Finds hidden risks, unvalidated assumptions, and missing dependencies — does not improve the spec. Returns APPROVED, NEEDS_REVISION, or BLOCKED with MoSCoW-classified findings. Conditionally invoked by the orchestrator based on risk/effort signals. ([wp-media/maestro](https://github.com/wp-media/maestro/blob/HEAD/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger**: Red-team reviewer for a spec or design before any code is written. Returns SOUND, or objections ranked BLOCKER / MAJOR / MINOR, each falsifiable with the simplest fix, plus the list of what it checked. Use after /cobot:spec, before tasks start, or on any plan the owner wants attacked. ([YpNo/cobot](https://github.com/YpNo/cobot/blob/HEAD/plugins/cobot/agents/challenger.md))
- **challenger-accessibility**: Read a design-system or frontend planning document and return an accessibility-focused pushback report — contrast violations, keyboard-trap risks, screen-reader gaps, colour-only signalling, missing focus management. Independent review that checks whether the plan can actually hit its stated a11y mandate. Use during `/sdcd:design-system-plan`, `/sdcd:frontend-plan`, and cross-cutting `/sdcd:audit`. ([Tschonsen/spec-driven-claude-development](https://github.com/Tschonsen/spec-driven-claude-development/blob/HEAD/plugin/sdcd/agents/challenger-accessibility.md))
- **challenger-advisor**: Decision Council dialectical challenger. Convened only when the board converges — steelmans the strongest rejected option, attacks the shared assumption the whole board leaned on, and names the blind spot common to every lens. Breaks isolation on purpose. Used by the decision-council skill. ([sash-rift/riftlab-personal-os](https://github.com/sash-rift/riftlab-personal-os/blob/HEAD/plugins/riftlab-os/agents/challenger-advisor.md) · ★1)
- **challenger-agy**: Invokes the agy (Antigravity) CLI pinned to a Gemini model from the debate workspace. Persona loaded from the workspace's agy/GEMINI.md context file. ([nalyk/nalyk-skills](https://github.com/nalyk/nalyk-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/debate/agents/challenger-agy.md) · ★2)
- **challenger-architect**: Challenger persona "architect" — a senior solution architect. Challenges how the things wanted are realised — the choices, their price, how the parts fit and what will break first. Not a document auditor. ([pche-broken-artist/forge-of-thought](https://github.com/pche-broken-artist/forge-of-thought/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger-architect.md) · ★15)
- **challenger-auditor**: &gt; **[独立盲审铁律]** &gt; 你运行在与初审审计员完全隔离的独立上下文中。你必须首先直接核对底层异常数据 (`rule_findings`、`anomaly_samples`、`sql_exploration`) 与法规证据链 (`evidence_chain`)，再审视初审结论 (`junior_report` 与 `junior_score`)： &gt; - 若初审审计员**漏报重大异常**、**低估舞弊风险**（如面对确凿拆单和周末大额入账却给出低于 60 分的风险评分）、**漏引关键法规**或**将实质违规美化为普通操作失误**，你必须予以严厉驳斥并大幅调高 `adjusted_risk_score`（当 $| \text{junior\_score} - \text{adjusted\_risk\_score} | &gt; 20$ 时，引擎将在证据图中自动生成 `conflict` ([Cyrus060224/auditcore-skill](https://github.com/Cyrus060224/auditcore-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/challenger-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **challenger-bear**: Adversarial bear-case reasoner for critical financial logic. Paired with challenger-bull in Agent Team pattern. Used for RSU hedge calc, debate-agent trade gate, Kelly sizing, tax loss harvest, options Greeks. Returns the strongest bear case + the best counter to a bull. ([hicksjoh/glastonbury-terminal](https://github.com/hicksjoh/glastonbury-terminal/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger-bear.md))
- **challenger-bull**: Adversarial bull-case reasoner for critical financial logic. Paired with challenger-bear in Agent Team pattern. Used for RSU hedge calc, debate-agent trade gate, Kelly sizing, tax loss harvest, options Greeks. Returns a strong bull argument + the strongest counter to a bear. ([hicksjoh/glastonbury-terminal](https://github.com/hicksjoh/glastonbury-terminal/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger-bull.md))
- **challenger-business-analyst**: Challenger persona "business-analyst" — a senior business analyst. Challenges whether the requirements say what the business needs and what was written from the parent though it belongs to the world outside it — the suppliers, the systems, the standards, the operations. Not a document auditor. ([pche-broken-artist/forge-of-thought](https://github.com/pche-broken-artist/forge-of-thought/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger-business-analyst.md) · ★15)
- **challenger-calibration**: Severity scoring guide with few-shot examples and hard thresholds for the challenger agent. ([martineserios/thebrana](https://github.com/martineserios/thebrana/blob/HEAD/system/agents/CALIBRATION.md) · ★3)
- **challenger-codex**: Invokes Codex CLI from the debate workspace. Persona loaded from the workspace AGENTS.md context file. ([nalyk/nalyk-skills](https://github.com/nalyk/nalyk-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/debate/agents/challenger-codex.md) · ★2)
- **challenger-cto**: Challenger persona "cto" — CTO-level peer reviewer. Challenges the substance of the principal's thinking — assumptions, blind spots, second-order effects, organisational reality. Not a document auditor. ([pche-broken-artist/forge-of-thought](https://github.com/pche-broken-artist/forge-of-thought/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/challenger-cto.md) · ★15)
- **challenger-maintainability**: Read a planning document (Ur-Plan / Backend-Plan / Frontend-Plan / Audit) and return a maintainability-focused pushback report — abstractions that will rot, cross-cutting concerns with unclear owners, naming drift, coupling that'll bite in 3 months. Independent review optimised for "future-you reading this cold." Use during any `/sdcd:*-plan` skill's challenger dispatch step. ([Tschonsen/spec-driven-claude-development](https://github.com/Tschonsen/spec-driven-claude-development/blob/HEAD/plugin/sdcd/agents/challenger-maintainability.md))
- **challenger-orchestrator**: Orchestrate Challenger sales-process agents across the lifecycle of a deal. Use when Alex says "orchestrate [account]", "run the Challenger play on [deal]", "what's the next move on [account]", "where am I on [deal]", "diagnose [account]", or when Alex is mid-cycle and unsure which Challenger micro-skill or agent to fire next. Triggers when a deal has multiple open questions (Reframe quality, Mobilizer status, pricing posture) and the right next move depends on stage. Decides which agent to invoke (reframe-architect, mobilizer-mapper, safe-bold-auditor, take-control-coach, commercial-insight-… ([AlexYedi/alex-agents-skills](https://github.com/AlexYedi/alex-agents-skills/blob/HEAD/GTM/Sales/Sales-Methodology-Skills/Challenger%20Sale/agents/challenger-orchestrator.md) · ★1)
- **challenger-performance**: Read a planning document (Ur-Plan / Backend-Plan / Frontend-Plan / Audit) and return a performance-focused pushback report — latency risks, N+1s, missing indexes, bundle-size traps, blocking calls. Independent review focused on what the success criteria actually demand. Use during any `/sdcd:*-plan` skill's challenger dispatch step.
